============================================
MONOPOLY-LK Simulation
============================================

Player 1 : Aggressive Investor
Player 2 : Conservative Banker
Player 3 : Risk Taker
Player 4 : Opportunistic Trader

Each player begins with LKR 30,000.

============================================
Determining the First Player
============================================

Aggressive Investor rolls 8.
Conservative Banker rolls 12.
Risk Taker rolls 8.
Opportunistic Trader rolls 4.

Aggressive Investor rolls 3.
Risk Taker rolls 6.

Conservative Banker will begin the game.

Turn order:
Conservative Banker
Risk Taker
Aggressive Investor
Opportunistic Trader
